### [94] [Collective depinning of dipolar skyrmion chains: thermal and athermal rounding of an elastic polymer in a quenched landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23181)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 用随机Thiele方程数值研究弱钉扎势中相互作用偶极斯格明子链的退钉扎动力学，揭示长链阈值、热致展宽、磁场依赖及约200个斯格明子链的内部形变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯格明子链而非孤立织构可作为信息载体，因此需理解其在弱无序钉扎和热涨落下的集体驱动与退钉扎行为，以评估器件可靠性。

Method: 求解随机Thiele方程，模拟不同长度、周期性边界的偶极斯格明子链在淬火吸引钉扎位点中的驱动，并改变磁场；以临界自旋电流表征退钉扎力。

Result: (i) 链长≥50时退钉扎阈值与长度无关，约为孤立斯格明子的1/3；(ii) 转变圆化仅部分热致：宽度从T=0的0.334±0.004 decades增至300 K的0.633±0.002，本征Larkin关联长度约2.6 bonds（kBT~V0/25以下）；(iii) 宽度在kBT~0.14 V0前几乎不变，到kBT~0.23 V0增大约四倍，主要源于creep区展宽，且与链长无关，排除有限尺寸圆化；磁场由30 mT增至40 mT时转变核心加宽26%、creep尾缩短；约200个斯格明子链的退钉扎经历内部形变序列：Larkin长度与粗糙度增长、瞬态键拉伸、随后迁移率和Hall角恢复。

Conclusion: 长链退钉扎阈值具有长度无关性和近孤立斯格明子1/3的特征；热涨落与磁场调控转变形状和creep行为；大链通过内部集体形变完成退钉扎，这些集体效应对以斯格明子链为信息载体的器件具有重要意义。

Abstract: The dynamics of interacting dipole skyrmion chains in a weak pinning landscape is studied through numerical solutions of the stochastic Thiele equation. Chains of different lengths with periodic boundary conditions are driven across quenched attractive pinning sites under different magnetic fields. A clear threshold spin current is identified as the critical depinning force. Three main results emerge: (i) for chains of 50 skyrmions or more, the depinning threshold is independent of chain length and remains close to one third of that of an isolated skyrmion. (ii) The transition rounding is only partly thermal: the width changes from w = 0.334 +/- 0.004 decades at T = 0 to 0.633 +/- 0.002 at 300 K, while the intrinsic Larkin correlation length remains about 2.6 bonds below kBT ~ V0/25. (iii) The width is nearly unchanged up to kBT ~ 0.14 V0, but increases almost fourfold by kBT ~ 0.23 V0, mainly through broadening of the creep regime. It is independent of chain length, ruling out finite-size rounding. Increasing the field from 30 to 40 mT changes the transition shape, widening its core by 26% while shortening the creep tails. For chains of about 200 skyrmions, depinning proceeds through a sequence of internal deformations - growth of the Larkin length and roughness, transient bond stretching, and subsequent recovery of mobility and Hall angle. These collective effects are relevant for skyrmion-based devices in which chains, rather than isolated textures, act as information carriers.

</details>


### [95] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling and magnetic chirality: Multidirectional spin polarization in a helical antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23618)
*Kallol Mondal,Sudin Ganguly,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在紧束缚框架下结合自旋轨道耦合，研究非共线螺旋反铁磁体中的自旋相关输运，并预测可由螺旋磁织构与自旋轨道耦合共同产生多方向自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋过滤系统通常主要产生单轴自旋极化；作者希望探索非共线磁序与自旋轨道耦合协同作用下能否产生多方向、可调控的自旋极化。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型描述非共线螺旋反铁磁体与自旋轨道耦合，并利用Landauer-Büttiker形式计算自旋相关输运与自旋极化。

Result: 发现可同时沿x、y、z方向产生有限自旋极化，区别于常规单轴自旋过滤；该多方向极化源于非共线磁序导致的对称性破缺。自旋轨道耦合显著改变极化，且强烈依赖螺旋系统参数；长程跃迁显著增强自旋过滤效应，使三个空间方向均出现大自旋极化分量。

Conclusion: 非共线螺旋反铁磁体中的螺旋磁织构、自旋轨道耦合与长程跃迁可协同实现多方向自旋极化，为可调控自旋过滤与自旋电子学器件提供机制。

Abstract: We investigate spin-dependent transport in a non-collinear helical antiferromagnet in the presence of spin-orbit coupling within a tight-binding framework. Using the Landauer-B\"{u}ttiker formalism, we analyze the generation of spin polarization arising from the combined effects of the helical magnetic texture and spin-orbit interaction. We find that finite spin polarization can be generated simultaneously along the $\hat{x}$, $\hat{y}$, and $\hat{z}$-directions, in contrast to the predominantly single-axis spin polarization commonly observed in conventional spin-filtering systems. The multidirectional spin polarization originates from the symmetry breaking introduced by the non-collinear magnetic order. We further show that the spin polarization is modified significantly in the presence of spin-orbit coupling and it has strong dependence on the parameters associated with the helical system. In particular, long-range hopping significantly enhances the spin-filtering effect, leading to large spin polarization components along all three spatial directions.

</details>


### [96] [Sub-Thermionic Switching in a Negative-Capacitance Bandgap-Change Field-Effect Transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23505)
*Rahul B Awale,Mike Klymenko,Yuefeng Yin,Nikhil V Medhekar,Bhaskaran Muralidharan,Michael S Fuhrer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出并理论模拟了负电容带隙变化场效应晶体管（NC-BCFET），利用铁电栅堆叠放大开启双层石墨烯带隙的电场，从而突破亚阈值摆幅的玻尔兹曼极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统负电容FET受电容匹配和沟道量子电容限制，难以持续降低亚阈值摆幅；低功耗纳米电子学仍需突破约60 mV/dec的极限。

Method: 构建自洽框架，耦合双层石墨烯的四带紧束缚哈密顿量、GW修正屏蔽、准静态Landau-Devonshire铁电响应以及弹道Landauer-Büttiker输运；采用Al0.55Sc0.45N作为负电容铁电层，双层石墨烯作为电可调带隙沟道。

Result: 负电容放大了层间电势差，使开关窗口相比介质栅对照压缩37倍；300 K优化器件亚阈值摆幅为15 mV/dec，低于玻尔兹曼极限约4倍，100 K时降至2.5 mV/dec；但室温开关比受双层石墨烯带隙限制仅约10^2。

Conclusion: NC-BCFET概念可直接推广到具有更大场可调带隙的材料，有望在室温同时实现深度亚热电子开关和高开关比。

Abstract: Overcoming the approximately 60~mV/dec Boltzmann limit of the subthreshold swing remains a central challenge for low-power nanoelectronics. Negative-capacitance field-effect transistors address it by amplifying the channel potential, but their effectiveness is constrained by capacitance matching and by the quantum capacitance of conventional channels. Here we model a negative-capacitance bandgap-change field-effect transistor (NC-BCFET), in which the ferroelectric gate stack instead amplifies the electric field that opens the bandgap of the channel, using experimentally proven materials: bilayer graphene as a channel with an electrically tunable bandgap and Al$_{0.55}$Sc$_{0.45}$N as the negative-capacitance ferroelectric. Our self-consistent framework couples a four-band tight-binding Hamiltonian with GW-corrected screening, a quasistatic Landau--Devonshire ferroelectric response, and ballistic Landauer--B\"uttiker transport. Negative capacitance amplifies the interlayer potential difference, compressing the switching window 37-fold relative to a dielectric-gated control. At 300~K, the optimized NC-BCFET achieves a subthreshold swing of 15~mV/dec, four times below the Boltzmann limit, improving to 2.5~mV/dec at 100~K. The room-temperature on/off ratio is limited to $\sim 10^{2}$ by the bandgap of bilayer graphene. The concept extends directly to materials with larger field-tunable bandgaps, where deep subthermionic switching and high on/off ratios can be achieved together at room temperature.

</details>


### [97] [Collective Coordinate Dynamics of Antiferromagnet: Gravity by Quantum Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24975)
*Kuangyin Deng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 反铁磁和亚铁磁织构的集体坐标动力学由 Néel 矢量 CP$^1$ 态量子几何支配，表现为测地线方程并产生等效黎曼引力与洛伦兹力；这与铁磁织构常用的 Thiele 方程根本不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁织构通常用无惯性的 Thiele 方程描述，缺乏引力类比；因此需要建立可刻画反铁磁/亚铁磁织构惯性与几何效应的新动力学框架。

Method: 将反铁磁与亚铁磁织构的集体坐标动力学映射为由 Néel 矢量 CP$^1$ 量子几何控制的测地线方程，从而导出等效 Riemannian 引力和织构运动中的洛伦兹力。

Result: 该框架成功解释了光驱动超快反铁磁畴壁实验中观测到的螺旋度依赖不对称运动，并预测畴壁宽度会发生剧烈呼吸式振荡。

Conclusion: 反铁磁和亚铁磁织构动力学呈现出与铁磁 Thiele 方程不同的物理图像，为理解其超快集体运动提供了量子几何与等效引力描述。

Abstract: The collective coordinate dynamics of antiferromagnetic and ferrimagnetic textures emerges as a geodesic equation governed by the quantum geometry of the CP$^1$ state associated with the N\'eel vector, rendering an effective Riemannian gravity and a Lorentz force in the texture motion. This unveils a physical scenario fundamentally distinct from the Thiele equation widely used for ferromagnetic textures, which is inertialess without any gravity analogy. Applying our framework to an optically driven ultrafast antiferromagnetic domain wall, we can explain the helicity-dependent asymmetric motion observed experimentally and predict a vibrantly breathing wall width.

</details>


### [98] [The Superconducting Talbot Effect in Phased-Array Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24572)
*Hechen Ren,Ziying Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出“超导塔尔博特效应”：近邻化库珀对在弹道二维电子气中传播时产生宏观量子自成像。通过相控阵约瑟夫森结主动调控超导塔尔博特地毯，并用游标式收集阵列进行亚波长采样，从而鲁棒地提取螺旋、自旋简并及自旋轨道劈裂费米面的费米波长；紧束缚计算验证了实空间干涉图样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导近邻库珀对在弹道二维电子气中的宏观量子自成像与相干波前调控，并克服传统输运测量分辨率限制，实现高鲁棒性的量子干涉成像和量子材料费米面诊断。

Method: 将周期性超导引线配置为具有可编程相位差的相控阵约瑟夫森结，以主动操控超导塔尔博特地毯；设计游标式收集阵列，对分数塔尔博特图样进行亚波长采样；在方格子模型上进行紧束缚数值计算以验证实空间干涉图样。

Result: 实现了超导塔尔博特地毯的主动转向；游标式亚波长采样可映射实空间量子干涉，且对无序具有高鲁棒性；可直接提取螺旋、自旋简并和自旋轨道劈裂费米面的费米波长；紧束缚数值结果与干涉图样一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超导光学中相干波前工程与量子材料诊断的通用框架，为基于超导近邻效应的量子干涉调控和费米面表征提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce the superconducting Talbot effect---a macroscopic quantum self-imaging phenomenon occurring when proximitized Cooper pairs propagate through a ballistic two-dimensional electron gas. By configuring periodic superconducting leads into a phased-array Josephson junction with programmable phase differences, we demonstrate active steering of the resulting superconducting Talbot carpet. To overcome transport resolution limits, we design a Vernier-scale collector array that performs sub-wavelength sampling of the fractional Talbot pattern. This approach maps real-space quantum interference with high robustness to disorder, enabling direct extraction of Fermi wavelengths across helical, spin-degenerate, and spin-orbit-split Fermi surfaces. Tight-binding numerical calculations on a square lattice validate the real-space interference patterns. Our results establish a versatile framework for coherent wavefront engineering and quantum materials diagnostics in superconducting optics.

</details>


### [99] [Vanadium doping induced valley asymmetries in WS$_2$ monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22580)
*Frederico B. Sousa,Boyang Zheng,Elizabeth Grace Houser,Paulo E. Faria Junior,Zhuohang Yu,Alessandra Ames,Gabriel A. D. Souza,Mingzu Liu,Gilmar Eugenio Marques,Leandro M. Malard,Mauricio Terrones,Vincent H. Crespi,Marcio D. Teodoro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在钒掺杂WS2单层中观察到谷不对称性：一个谷具有更大的塞曼斜率和圆偏振度，且掺杂样品的总圆偏振度约为原始WS2单层的两倍；DFT计算表明自旋依赖杂化导致导带边能量偏移不同，从而造成谷间激子能量差异，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD单层可通过谷自由度编码和操控信息，但实际应用仍需更好地控制谷极化效率和谷塞曼效应；此前研究表明，引入自旋极化金属原子作为替代缺陷可破坏TMD单层的时间反演对称性，诱导室温铁磁序并增强谷依赖光学响应。

Method: 研究钒掺杂WS2单层的谷不对称性，在给定磁极化下比较两个谷的塞曼斜率和圆偏振度，并结合密度泛函理论计算分析掺杂结构中的能带与激子能量变化。

Result: 给定磁极化时，一个谷表现出比另一个谷更大的塞曼斜率和圆偏振度；掺杂样品的整体圆偏振度约为原始WS2单层的两倍。DFT计算显示，自旋依赖杂化导致导带边能量移动不同，进而使两个谷的激子能量不同，与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了通过缺陷工程调控TMD单层谷相关光学响应的可行性，为基于缺陷工程二维材料的谷电子学技术提供了新途径。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers offer an innovative platform for encoding and manipulating information through the valley degree of freedom. While unique valley-related physical phenomena have been reported so far, practical applications still require advanced control over the valley polarization efficiency and the valley Zeeman effect. Recently, the introduction of spin-polarized metal atoms as substitutional defects was reported to break the time-reversal symmetry in TMD monolayers, consequently inducing a room-temperature ferromagnetic ordering and enhancing the valley-dependent optical responses. Here, we report valley asymmetries for vanadium-doped WS$_2$ monolayers. With a given magnetic polarization, one valley exhibits larger Zeeman slope and degree of circular polarization than the other valley. Additionally, the overall degree of circular polarization in the doped samples is approximately twice that of the pristine WS$_2$ monolayer. Density functional theory calculations in the doped structure show that different energy shifts in conduction band edges due to spin-dependent hybridization lead to different exciton energies between valleys, which is consistent with the experimental observations. Our results pave the way for valleytronic technologies based on defect-engineered two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [100] [Electrical Probing of Dark Excitons through Microwave Permittivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22710)
*Alex Stram,Zhida Liu,Ziheng Zhang,Yangchen He,Xuejian Ma,Kyoung Pyo Lee,Lisa Frammolino,Fuxiang Chen,Robert J. Boyd,Sirapas Tangton,Anand Swain,Kan Yao,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yuebing Zheng,Chih-Kang Shih,Daniel Rhodes,Li Yang,Xiaoqin Li,Keji Lai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用激光照明微波阻抗显微镜，在单层WS2中观测到光激发激子产生的GHz可调介电响应，实现暗激子的纳米尺度无接触电学成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子寿命短，通常只能通过光学信号探测，被认为难以电学检测；暗激子更缺乏直接探测与输运成像手段。

Method: 采用激光照明微波阻抗显微镜测量单层二硫化钨的GHz介电响应，对比高纯封装与缺陷样品，并结合空间扩散映射、亚线性功率依赖分析和第一性原理激子极化率建模。

Result: 高纯封装样品表现出与激子谱共振的纯介电响应，缺陷样品则由常规光电导主导；长寿命暗激子是主要贡献者，第一性原理模型复现了测量到的极化率/介电响应。

Conclusion: 激子可作为光学可调介电元件；微波显微镜成为具有亚100 nm分辨率的暗激子输运直接电学探针。

Abstract: Excitons in atomically thin semiconductors are almost always probed through their optical signatures, because the short lifetimes of these transient quasiparticles are generally assumed to preclude electrical detection. Here we show that photoexcited excitons in monolayer tungsten disulfide produce a large, optically tunable permittivity at gigahertz frequencies, and that the effect provides a contact-free electrical route to imaging dark excitons at the nanoscale. Using laser-illuminated microwave impedance microscopy, we find that high-purity encapsulated flakes exhibit a purely dielectric response resonant with the exciton spectrum, whereas defect-rich samples are governed by conventional photoconductivity. Spatial mapping of diffusion and sublinear power dependence identify long-lived dark excitons as the dominant contributors, and first-principles modelling of exciton polarizability reproduces the measured susceptibility. Our results establish excitons as optically tunable dielectric elements and introduce microwave microscopy as a direct electrical probe of dark-exciton transport with sub-100 nm resolution.

</details>


### [101] [Non-Hermitian Topology from Edge Transport in Hermitian Quantum Anomalous Hall Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23099)
*Humian Zhou,Ming Lu,Chui-Zhen Chen,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文表明，非厄米输运可无需外部增益/损耗工程，而作为全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统的内禀输运响应自然出现；其由手性边缘模式与扩散边缘模式耦合诱导，表现为连续Hatano-Nelson模型及可观测的非厄米趋肤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米物理中的例外点和趋肤效应此前主要在依赖受控增益/损耗的工程系统中实现；作者希望探究其在无需外部非厄米工程、且整体厄米的凝聚态/介观系统中是否可自然产生。

Method: 分析全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统中单向手性边缘模式与扩散型普通边缘模式的相互作用，将其映射为连续Hatano-Nelson模型，并用Landauer–Büttiker模拟检验输运特征；提出磁性拓扑绝缘体作为实现平台。

Result: 该耦合导致本征非互易输运和非厄米趋肤效应：电化学势与局域热耗散呈手性依赖的指数空间分布，纵向电导随系统尺寸指数衰减，而霍尔电导保持量子化；模拟确认了这些霍尔条可观测信号。

Conclusion: 结果将非厄米拓扑与介观输运联系起来，表明磁性拓扑绝缘体等固态平台可实现内禀非厄米拓扑输运，为固态非厄米拓扑器件开辟路径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian physics, known for exotic phenomena like exceptional points and the skin effect, has been most prominently realized in engineered systems relying on controlled gain and loss. Here we show that it can also arise naturally as an intrinsic transport response of a globally Hermitian quantum anomalous Hall system, without the need for external non-Hermitian engineering. We show that the interplay between unidirectional chiral edge modes and diffusive normal edge modes induces intrinsic non-reciprocal transport described by a continuum Hatano-Nelson model. Consequently, the non-Hermitian skin effect is encoded directly in experimentally accessible Hall-bar observables: the electrochemical potential and local heat dissipation acquire chirality-dependent exponential spatial profiles, while the longitudinal conductance decays exponentially with system size and the Hall conductance remains quantized. Using Landauer--B{"u}ttiker simulations, we confirm these transport signatures and identify magnetic topological insulators as a realistic platform for an intrinsic non-Hermitian transport response. Our results bridge non-Hermitian topology with mesoscopic transport, opening a pathway toward non-Hermitian topological devices in solid-state systems.

</details>


### [102] [Magnetic Quadrupole Lens and Current-Induced Quadrupolar Dynamics of Antiskyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23129)
*Linfeng Du,Yang Liu,Peng Yan,Ying Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 反斯格明子可作为磁四极透镜，对穿过它的电流进行聚焦或散焦；其核心在特定注入方向成为电子束汇聚焦点，导致各向异性且非均匀的电流分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电流驱动反斯格明子动力学通常受自旋转移矩影响，但反斯格明子自身对电流的透镜效应及其与电子输运的耦合尚未被充分揭示；该效应有望解决反斯格明子赛道存储器中的关键挑战。

Method: 结合量子输运模拟与微磁模拟进行迭代，自洽地描述传导电子与反斯格明子的耦合动力学。

Result: 发现四极反斯格明子霍尔效应：其强烈依赖电流方向，并可通过自旋轨道耦合有效调控。电流聚焦/散焦会显著提高/抑制反斯格明子速度，同时降低其霍尔角。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了本征电流透镜效应作为操控拓扑磁结构的有力机制，并为基于反斯格明子的自旋电子器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that antiskyrmions can serve as magnetic quadrupole lenses that focus or defocus electric currents traversing them. We showcase the antiskyrmion core as a focal point where electron beams converge for specific injection directions, leading to anisotropic and nonuniform current distributions. Thus the current-induced antiskyrmion dynamics is profoundly modified by the magnetic quadrupole lensing via the spin-transfer torque. By incorporating quantum transport and micromagnetic simulations iteratively, we self-consistently capture the coupled dynamics of conduction electrons and antiskyrmions. Our results unveil a quadrupolar antiskyrmion Hall effect that is highly anisotropic with respect to the current direction and can be effectively tuned by the spin-orbit coupling. In particular, the current focusing (defocusing) dramatically enhances (suppresses) the antiskyrmion speed while simultaneously reducing its Hall angle, thereby overcoming a key challenge for antiskyrmion racetrack memories. Our work establishes the intrinsic current lensing as a powerful mechanism for manipulating topological magnetic textures and opens new avenues for antiskyrmion-based spintronic devices.

</details>


### [103] [Non-Markovian Quantum Dynamics of Exciton-Polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23295)
*Rajanya Sarkar,Pritha Ghosh,Arshath Manjalingal,David R. Reichman,Arkajit Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种非马尔可夫主方程方法，可对激子-极化激元非平衡动力学进行全量子模拟，并在TIPS-pentacene中再现由声子诱导非马尔可夫Lamb位移导致的极化激元群速度重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子-极化激元可在室温下表现介观相干量子传播，但精确的全量子动力学模拟计算代价过高，传统半经典近似限制了对声子、光子与电子在集体光-物质耦合下复杂动力学相互作用的微观理解。

Method: 发展非马尔可夫主方程框架，超越马尔可夫极限以刻画声子诱导的退相干与耗散；通过波矢空间粗粒化，并并行演化密度矩阵各对角元，从而实现非平衡激子-极化激元动力学的全量子模拟。

Result: 在TIPS-pentacene激子-极化激元输运模拟中，该方法合理捕捉实验观测到的极化激元群速度重整化，其来源为声子诱导的非马尔可夫Lamb位移；同时表明传统马尔可夫理论无法再现该重整化。

Conclusion: 该非马尔可夫全量子方法为研究激子-极化激元输运提供了可行且有效的微观模拟途径，并凸显非马尔可夫效应在解释极化激元群速度重整化及相关光-物质耦合动力学中的关键作用。

Abstract: Exciton-polaritons, hybrid light-matter quasiparticles formed when a material interacts with a confined electric field, have experimentally been shown to exhibit mesoscale coherent quantum propagation that remains robust at room temperature. However, an accurate and direct quantum dynamical simulation of this phenomenon that does not resort to semi-classical approximations is prohibitively expensive computationally, limiting the microscopic understanding of the rich dynamical interplay among phonons, photons, and electrons under collective light-matter coupling. To address this fundamental challenge, we develop a non-Markovian master equation approach which enables the fully quantum mechanical simulation of non-equilibrium exciton-polariton dynamics and captures phonon-induced decoherence and dissipation beyond the conventional Markovian limit. To carry out this task, a procedure is developed in which the wave vector space is coarse-grained and each diagonal element of the density matrix is evolved in parallel. To demonstrate the utility of this approach, we simulate exciton-polariton transport in TIPS-pentacene. We find that our approach reasonably captures the experimentally observed renormalization of the polariton group velocity, which originates from the phonon-induced non-Markovian Lamb shift. We further show that this renormalization cannot be reproduced within conventional Markovian theories.

</details>


### [104] [Non-Hermitian Quantum Mechanics I: Instantaneous Self-Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23426)
*Lingfeng Liu,Wei-Wei Yang,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 从闭量子系统幺正演化出发，严格证明任意局域子系统的投影波函数满足精确的含时非厄米薛定谔方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子系统中有效非厄米动力学的出现提供严格的微观基础，并克服传统非厄米近似缺乏严格推导的问题。

Method: 将全局波函数投影到任意局域子系统，构造精确的时间局域生成元；核心是含时且一般非厄米的瞬时自能，并系统对比传统非厄米近似与该精确框架。

Result: 得到不依赖Born近似、Markov近似和宽带极限的精确非厄米方程；瞬时自能编码环境反作用，并与子系统哈密顿量共同决定投影动力学；揭示传统非厄米近似的局限性。

Conclusion: 该工作为有效非厄米动力学提供了严格的微观基础，并明确其精确成立条件与传统近似的适用范围。

Abstract: Starting from the unitary evolution of a closed quantum system, we rigorously demonstrate that the projection of the global wavefunction onto an arbitrary local subsystem is governed by an exact, time-dependent non-Hermitian Schr\"odinger equation. Crucially, the derivation does not rely on conventional approximations such as the Born approximation, the Markov approximation, or the wide-band limit. The central quantity is the \textit{instantaneous self-energy}, a time-dependent and generally non-Hermitian operator that encodes environmental backaction and, together with the subsystem Hamiltonian, forms the exact time-local generator of the projected dynamics. By benchmarking the conventional non-Hermitian approximation against this exact framework, we systematically expose its limitations. These results provide a rigorous microscopic foundation for the emergence of effective non-Hermitian dynamics in quantum systems.

</details>


### [105] [Universal Generalized Brillouin Zone Theory I: Review of the Spectral Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23523)
*Zeqi xu,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本论文是非厄米高维广义布里渊区（GBZ）理论系列的第一篇，指出现有1D GBZ理论并不完整，并系统梳理1D谱方法以引出Paper II的波函数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统1D GBZ条件 |β_p|=|β_{p+1}| 在2D中已知失效，但从1D到2D过渡时该失效如何发生尚不清楚；回答此问题反而揭示出既有1D GBZ理论本身仍有欠缺。

Method: 系统回顾1D谱方法，分析其推导框架、基本假设及内在困难，厘清传统1D GBZ条件在向2D推广时出现问题的根源。

Result: 发现现有1D GBZ理论存在不完整性，并识别出推动后续波函数方法发展的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本工作为Paper II发展波函数方法提供动机与问题基础，是建立普适高维非厄米系统GBZ理论的起点。

Abstract: This series of papers aims to establish a universal generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) theory for higher-dimensional non-Hermitian systems. As the starting point of this series, we emphasize a fundamental question: while the conventional one-dimensional (1D) GBZ condition, $|\beta_p| = |\beta_{p+1}|$, is well known to fail in two dimensions, how does this breakdown actually occur as a system gradually crosses over from 1D to 2D? Investigating this question reveals that the existing 1D GBZ theory itself remains incomplete. In this first paper, we therefore systematically review the 1D spectral approach and clarify where the underlying difficulties lie. Our work identifies the key challenges that motivate the wavefunction approach developed in Paper~II.

</details>


### [106] [Multiscale Modeling of Ion Transport in Nanopores: Fitting Implicit-Water Radial Diffusion Profiles to Explicit-Water Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23575)
*Mónika Valiskó,Salman Shabbir,Eszter Molnárné Lakics,Zoltán Ható,Dezső Boda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种多尺度方法，将从显式水分子动力学模拟得到的分子尺度传输信息转化为高效纳米孔离子传输模型中的有效扩散系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算高效模型常采用空间恒定扩散系数和平均场近似，难以描述孔壁附近离子迁移率强抑制、离子相关性及多价离子特异性结合等效应。

Method: 在NP+LEMC框架中，将Nernst--Planck方程与局部平衡Monte Carlo结合，以计入超出平均场的离子相关性；用显式水MD获得的径向电导率剖面拟合径向变化的有效扩散系数剖面，并应用于带负电二氧化硅纳米孔中的NaCl、CaCl2及其混合物。

Result: 拟合得到的扩散系数剖面可重现MD的径向电流分布，尤其能刻画孔壁附近离子迁移率的强抑制。NaCl表现为阳离子选择性，源于近壁Na+传导增强；CaCl2表现为弱阴离子选择性，因强结合的Ca2+在表面附近迁移率被强烈抑制；NaCl--CaCl2混合物中Ca2+优先结合导致离子电导呈非线性变化。

Conclusion: 该方法在实验相关器件行为与计算高效简化模型之间建立了桥梁，显式水MD提供分子尺度信息并可纳入有效传输系数。

Abstract: We develop a multiscale approach for incorporating molecular-scale transport information into computationally efficient models of ion transport through nanopores. A radially varying effective diffusion coefficient profile is fitted to radial conductivity profiles obtained from explicit-water molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. The fitting is performed within the NP+LEMC framework, which combines the Nernst--Planck equation with Local Equilibrium Monte Carlo to account for ion correlations beyond mean-field approximation. We apply the approach to NaCl, CaCl$_2$, and their mixtures in a negatively charged silica nanopore. The resulting diffusion coefficient profiles reproduce the radial current distributions of the MD simulations, including the strong suppression of ionic mobility near the pore wall that cannot be captured by a spatially constant diffusion coefficient inside the pore. NaCl and CaCl$_2$ exhibit qualitatively different transport behavior: the former is cation selective due to enhanced near-wall Na$^+$ conduction, whereas the latter shows weak anion selectivity because strongly bound Ca$^{2+}$ ions have strongly suppressed mobility near the surface. For NaCl--CaCl$_2$ mixtures, preferential Ca$^{2+}$ binding leads to nonlinear changes in ionic conductance. The approach establishes a bridge between experimentally relevant device behavior and computationally efficient reduced models, with explicit-water MD providing the molecular-scale information that can be incorporated into the effective transport coefficients.

</details>


### [107] [Scalable Multiple Electron Transport Architectures for Feedback Traceable Current Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24015)
*Agustin Javier Lapi,Guillermo Fernandez Moroni,Fernando Chierchie,Fabricio Alcalde Bessia,Miqueas Ezequiel Gamero,Brenda Aurea Cervantes Vergara,Blas Junior Irigoyen Gimenez,Eduardo Paolini,Claudio Rodrigo Chavez Blanco,Juan Estrada,Javier Tiffenberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种基于浮栅架构的可扩展电子计数电流单元，作为多电子输运器件（MET），实现离散电荷包的可控注入、输运与精确定量，并用于可电子溯源的电流源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要紧凑、可扩展且可精确控制的量子化电流源，以服务电子计数电流源与精密量子计量，并降低读出噪声。

Method: 利用浮栅MET的非破坏性多次读出能力与电荷注入技术，对离散电荷包进行受控产生、输运和计数；同时探索并联与串联多放大器可扩展架构。

Result: 实验证实噪声随独立测量数目的平方根降低，并实现稳定、可编程的输出电流。

Conclusion: 该方案为电子计数电流源和精密量子计量应用提供了一种紧凑且可扩展的平台。

Abstract: We present a scalable electron-counting current cell based on a floating gate architecture operated as a Multiple Electron Transport device (MET). The system enables controlled injection, transport, and precise quantification of discrete charge packets using the sensor non-destructive multiple readout capability. We repurposed the charge-injection technique, jointly with the electron-resolution capability, for a controlled generation of quantized charge packets for an electron-traceable current source.
  Scalable architectures based on parallel and series multi-amplifier configurations are explored. Experimental results confirm noise reduction following the square root of the number of independent measurements and demonstrate stable, programmable output current. This approach provides a compact and scalable platform for electron-counting current sources and precision quantum metrology applications.

</details>


### [108] [Corbino-Enhanced Supersonic Acoustic-Emission Threshold in a GaAs Two-Dimensional Electron System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24585)
*A. D. Levin,A. S. Jaroshevich,Z. D. Kvon,V. A. Chitta,M. S. Aksenov,D. V. Dmitriev,A. K. Bakarov,G. M. Gusev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Corbino几何的高迁移率GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子气中测量非线性微分电阻，发现零磁场下负电流支存在极性选择性阈值峰，并随垂直磁场减弱；该异常被归因于内接触附近径向电流集中引发的类Cherenkov声学声子发射阈值，极性选择源于Peltier加热/冷却。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究高迁移率二维电子系统中的局域非平衡电子-声子过程，并检验在已独立建立的流体力学交叉区中，Corbino几何能否通过径向电流集中驱动局域电子速度超过声速。

Method: 对Corbino图案化的GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子系统进行非线性微分电阻R_diff(I_dc)测量，并施加垂直磁场；结合径向电流密度j_r(r)=I/(2πr)分析内接触附近的局域电子速度、声速阈值及热边界条件。

Result: 零磁场下R_diff(I_dc)在负电流支出现显著极性选择性阈值峰，垂直磁场逐渐抑制该峰；该Corbino异常被解释为内接触附近局域类Cherenkov声学声子发射阈值，而极性选择性主要由Peltier加热与冷却调制接触附近热边界条件所致，而非运动学阈值本身。

Conclusion: Corbino几何可作为探测高迁移率电子系统中局域非平衡电子-声子过程的灵敏平台，并表明内Corbino接触可充当几何定义的超声声发射源。

Abstract: We report nonlinear differential-resistance measurements in a high-mobility GaAs/AlGaAs two-dimensional electron system patterned in a Corbino geometry. At zero magnetic field, $R_{\mathrm{diff}}(I_{dc})$ exhibits a pronounced polarity-selective threshold peak on the negative-current branch, gradually suppressed by a perpendicular magnetic field. We interpret the Corbino anomaly as a local Cherenkov-like threshold for acoustic phonon emission, enabled by the radial current concentration $j_r(r)=I/(2\pi r)$, which drives the local electron velocity above the sound velocity in a region adjacent to the inner contact. The polarity selectivity is attributed to Peltier heating and cooling, which modify the local thermal and boundary conditions near the contact, rather than to the kinematic threshold itself. Our results identify the Corbino geometry as a sensitive platform for probing local nonequilibrium electron--phonon processes in a high-mobility electron system within an independently established hydrodynamic-crossover regime and suggest that the inner Corbino contact can act as a geometry-defined source for supersonic acoustic emission.

</details>


### [109] [Observation of Kondo Effect in Rhombohedral Graphene Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24734)
*Xin Liao,Qing Yin,Huiwen Wang,Jing-Wei Dong,Jun-Xi Chen,Si-Li Wu,Cai-Zhen Li,Guowei Lyu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wei Jiang,Yu-Gui Yao,Zhi-Min Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在六层菱面石墨烯莫尔超晶格中实验观测到可调的Kondo相互作用：磁场或温度破坏Kondo单态，导致磁输运异常及向重费米液体转变，并暗示Kondo绝缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo效应源于传导电子与局域磁矩的反铁磁耦合，可产生多种奇异量子现象；二维莫尔超晶格提供拓扑平带，其中Wannier轨道受周期势空间限制而作为局域磁矩，构成可观测Kondo效应的可调平台。

Method: 采用六层菱面石墨烯莫尔超晶格，进行磁输运和温度依赖测量，并系统改变磁场、温度、载流子密度n与位移电场D。

Result: 随磁场增大，磁电阻跨临界场B_c呈现先增后减，Hall电阻R_xy在B_c附近符号反转；降温时R_xx先对数增加后按T^2下降，表明向重费米液体转变。这些行为可由磁场或温度诱导Kondo单态崩溃解释：释放原先束缚于局域磁矩的载流子，增强电导并改变主导载流子类型；Kondo相互作用可由n和D连续调控，并暗示Kondo绝缘态出现。

Conclusion: 研究深入揭示了莫尔工程平带系统中的Kondo效应，并为探索奇异关联量子相提供了新路径。

Abstract: Kondo effect in strongly correlated systems arises from the antiferromagnetic coupling between itinerant conduction electrons and localized magnetic moments, giving rise to a variety of exotic quantum phenomena. Two-dimensional moir\'e superlattice systems provide a highly tunable platform featuring topological flat bands, where Wannier orbitals are spatially confined by the periodic moir\'e potential and serve as localized magnetic moments, enabling the observable Kondo effect. Here we experimentally demonstrate Kondo interactions in hexalayer rhombohedral graphene moir\'e superlattices through magneto-transport and temperature-dependent measurements. With increasing magnetic field, the magnetoresistance exhibits an increase-decrease transition across a critical field B_c, while the Hall resistance R_xy undergoes a sign reversal near B_c. Moreover, as temperature decreases, the longitudinal resistance R_xx first increases logarithmically and then decreases following a T^2 behavior, indicating a transition to heavy fermion liquid. These behaviors can be consistently explained by the breakdown of Kondo singlets induced by either magnetic field or temperature, which liberates carriers previously bound to localized moments, thereby enhancing conductivity and altering the dominant carrier type. Furthermore, our results demonstrate that the Kondo interaction can be continuously tuned by both carrier density n and displacement electric field D, and suggest the emergence of a Kondo insulating state. Our findings provide deep insight into the Kondo effect in moir\'e engineered flat-band systems, paving the path for exploring exotic correlated quantum phases.

</details>


### [110] [Modified Low-Temperature Scanning Tunneling Microscope for Ultrafast Pump-Probe Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24916)
*Vibhuti N. Rai,Junyoung Sim,Nils Bogdanoff,Sergey Trishin,Florian Faaber,Paul Wiechers,Caroline Firschke,Tobias Kampfrath,Christian Lotze,Katharina J. Franke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过仅微调低温辐射屏蔽，改造 Besocke“Beetle”型 STM，在低于 6 K 下实现高效 THz/光脉冲耦合，并获得原子分辨的 THz 诱导隧穿电流成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THz-STM 可在原子级空间和亚皮秒时间分辨下探测声子、自旋和载流子超快动力学，但将 THz 辐射耦合进低温 STM 结面临光学接入与结稳定性兼顾的技术挑战；本文希望避免设计全新 STM 头部和定制真空内光学系统。

Method: 对 Besocke“Beetle”型 STM 进行最小改动，主要调整低温辐射屏蔽；在独立激光台上用飞秒激光在 LiNbO3 晶体中产生 THz 脉冲，并将 THz 与光脉冲耦合到 STM 结，同时保持温度低于 6 K；通过互相关测量确定有效时间分辨，并测量 Au(111) 上单层 MoS2 的 THz 诱导隧穿电流。

Result: 实现了在给定几何条件下的高效 THz 和光脉冲耦合，温度低于 6 K；给出了获得的 THz 脉冲波形并通过互相关确定有效时间分辨；THz 诱导隧穿电流图显示原子级对比，并可分辨一个点缺陷，表明 THz 耦合高效且低温 STM 运行稳定。

Conclusion: 无需定制全新 STM 头部，仅对现有低温 Besocke 型 STM 做最小改动即可实现高效 THz 耦合、低于 6 K 稳定运行和原子分辨成像，为 THz-STM 实验提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Combining scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) with terahertz pulses (THz-STM) enables ultrafast dynamics of phonons, spins, and charge carriers to be probed with atomic-scale spatial and sub-picosecond temporal resolution. However, coupling THz radiation into a low-temperature STM junction presents some technical challenges as it requires optical access for laser illumination while maintaining junction stability. Instead of designing a completely new STM head with customized optical access and optics within the ultra-high vacuum chamber, we modified a Besocke "Beetle" style STM with minimal changes of the cryogenic radiation shields. Despite the given geometrical conditions, we achieved efficient coupling of THz and optical pulses to the STM junction while maintaining temperatures below 6 K. We designed the optical setup on a separate laser table, starting with femtosecond laser pulses that are used to generate THz pulses in a LiNbO$_3$ crystal. We show the obtained THz pulse shapes and determine the effective time resolution using cross-correlation measurements. We further demonstrate the stable performance of the modified STM by measuring THz-induced tunneling currents from monolayer molybdenum disulfide (MoS$_2$) grown on Au(111). The resulting current maps resolve atomic-scale contrast revealing a point defect. The measurements confirm efficient THz coupling and stable low-temperature STM operation with atomic resolution.

</details>


### [111] [Model of the magnon Kerr effect in a highly anisotropic ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24959)
*Davit Petrosyan,Hiroki Matsumoto,Hanchen Wang,Richard Schlitz,Pietro Gambardella,William Legrand*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究了具有单轴各向异性薄膜中的磁子克尔效应，推导了其哈密顿量，给出不同外场取向下克尔系数，并建立混合腔-磁子系统运动方程以计算微波透射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁子克尔效应是有限磁各向异性铁磁体中普遍存在的非线性现象，表现为磁子色散关系随磁子布居数变化；需要理论刻画薄膜中的该非线性及其实验可观测性。

Method: 针对单轴各向异性薄膜推导磁子克尔效应哈密顿量；分别考虑外场平行、垂直及与各向异性轴成中间角的情形，确定克尔系数；分析非线性对不同磁性材料随磁子频率和样品体积的标度；进一步推导含薄膜磁子克尔非线性的混合腔-磁子系统运动方程，并得到光子/磁子布居及微波透射的散射矩阵项。

Result: 得到了各外场取向下薄膜磁子克尔系数的表达式，揭示了非线性随磁子频率和磁样品体积的标度规律，并导出了混合腔-磁子系统中光子与磁子布居及微波透射相关散射矩阵项。

Conclusion: 该工作为薄膜磁子克尔非线性提供了系统理论描述，并为相关腔-磁子混合系统的实验观测与微波透射分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The magnon Kerr effect is a nonlinear phenomenon that occurs universally in any ferromagnet with finite magnetic anisotropy. It manifests itself as a magnon population-dependent change in the magnon dispersion relation. Here, we derive the Hamiltonian for the magnon Kerr effect for a thin film with uniaxial anisotropy. We consider the external field, parallel, perpendicular, and at an intermediate angle to the anisotropy axis, and determine the Kerr coefficient in each case. We show how the nonlinearity scales for different magnetic materials as a function of magnon frequency and magnetic sample volume. Moreover, we derive the equations of motion for a hybrid cavity-magnon system with such thin-film magnon Kerr nonlinearity, to obtain populations of photons and magnons, and scattering matrix terms for the microwave transmission of the cavity, relevant for experimental observations.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Benchmarking higher-ranking multipoles and polarizability tensors for small molecular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24606)
*Bruno V. von Bruening,Alston J. Misquitta*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为73个小分子的偶极/四极矩及偶极-偶极和四极-四极极化率提供CCSD(T)参考值，系统评估HF、MP2、CCSD及多种DFT方法，并给出基组要求与推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确分子性质对构建分子间相互作用模型至关重要，且需超越领头阶偶极项，纳入四极等更高阶性质。

Method: 以73个非自旋极化小分子的CCSD(T)结果为参考，计算分子偶极矩、四极矩、偶极-偶极极化率和四极-四极极化率；对比HF、MP2、CCSD及大量DFT方法，并研究同时描述这些性质所需的基组水平。

Result: 表现最好的DFT泛函是渐近校正杂化GGA：B97-3-AC，其次是PBE0-AC；现代meta-GGA和range-separated泛函表现不稳定，在四极性质上误差较大。对DFT，Jensen aug-pcseg-2是较高效替代基组；对相关参考值，第三周期元素受益于核心极化，而四极-四极极化率严格需要双重增广。

Conclusion: 结果对基于DFT的对称适应微扰理论（SAPT(DFT)）等分子间相互作用方法以及机器学习数据集中的性质与相互作用质量具有直接影响，并为方法/基组选择提供依据。

Abstract: Molecular properties govern how molecules interact with one another or external fields. Accurate molecular properties are essential for constructing intermolecular interaction models, and to achieve high accuracy we need to go beyond leading-order (dipolar) terms.
  This work presents CCSD(T) references for 73 small non-spin-polarized molecules for molecular dipole and quadrupole moments, and dipole-dipole and quadrupole-quadrupole polarizabilities. Using these results, we provide a holistic performance analysis of molecular properties computed with HF, MP2, CCSD, and a wide range of density-functional methods. Additionally, we investigate in detail what levels of basis sets are required to simultaneously describe all properties in the data set.
  The best-performing density functionals for all four properties are the asymptotically cor- rected hybrid GGAs B97-3-AC, closely followed by PBE0-AC. Surprisingly, modern meta- GGAs and range-separated functionals perform inconsistently, with large errors in the quadrupo- lar properties. These results have direct implications for methods for intermolecular interac- tions, such as symmetry-adapted perturbation theory based on density functional theory, or for the quality of properties and interactions in machine-learning datasets.
  Finally, for DFT methods, the Jensen aug-pcseg-2 basis set is a computationally efficient alternative to more established basis sets, but for generating correlated references, third-row elements benefit from core polarization, and double augmentation is strictly necessary for quadrupole-quadrupole polarizabilities.

</details>


### [113] [XC100: A Wavefunction-Derived Exchange-Correlation Energy Dataset for Atomic and Molecular Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22490)
*Vaibhav Khanna,Paul M. Zimmerman*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一套从波函数构建精确Kohn-Sham交换关联能的流程，并生成包含100个闭壳层主族原子/分子的XC100数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为密度泛函近似的评估与开发提供高质量、系统的交换关联参考数据，需要能跨原子和分子物种获得准确的波函数衍生KS XC能量。

Method: 在cc-pVTZ基组下用组态相互作用波函数获得相关总能和密度，并将每个波函数密度映射到KS行列式；再通过加入cc-pVQZ价层基组和cc-pCVTZ芯层基组，并结合两点Riemann外推逼近完全基组极限，形成复合校正。

Result: 将该流程应用于100个主族闭壳层原子和分子构建XC100数据集；所得KS密度能密切复现相关密度，L2/Ne中位差为1.42×10^{-5}，复合校正恢复了cc-pVTZ参考之外的大量相关能；与常规及机器学习密度泛函近似比较，可洞察不同XC模型类型的Exc质量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一条生成XC参考数据的实用路径，可用于密度泛函的评估与开发。

Abstract: A workflow is introduced for constructing accurate, wavefunction-derived Kohn-Sham (KS) exchange-correlation (XC) energies across atomic and molecular species. Correlated total energies and densities are obtained from configuration interaction wave functions in a polarized triple-zeta basis, cc-pVTZ, and each wavefunction density is then mapped onto a KS determinant. A composite correction adds valence and core basis functions (cc-pVQZ and cc-pCVTZ), plus a two-point Riemann extrapolation to approach the complete-basis-set limit. The workflow is applied to 100 closed-shell atomic and molecular species composed of main group elements to form the XC100 data set. The resulting KS densities closely reproduce the correlated densities, with a median L2/Ne difference of 1.42 X 10^{-5}, while the composite corrections recover substantial correlation energy beyond the cc-pVTZ reference. Comparison with conventional and machine-learned density functional approximations gives insight into the quality of Exc across different types of models for XC. In all, this paper's workflow demonstrates a practical route for generating XC reference data for the assessment and development of density functionals.

</details>


### [114] [SPIBER: Reconstructing Free Energy Landscapes from Short, Unconverged Trajectories with Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22663)
*Venkata Sai Sreyas Adury,Pratyush Tiwary*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: SPIBER结合SPIB与GFlowNets，从有限未偏置短轨迹中学习低维慢变量并估计亚稳态间自由能差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子体系自由度极高，但亚稳行为常可由少量集体变量描述；然而短轨迹往往未跨越亚稳态，无法获得平衡布居，且同温同Hamiltonian下基于直方图重加权的方法也难以校正采样不平衡。

Method: SPIB通过过去-未来信息瓶颈深度学习逼近慢自由度，保留预测未来亚稳态所需信息；该压缩限制已采样区域的条件熵变化，使条件平均势能可近似自由能差。随后以这些能量定义GFlowNets的目标分布，用基于能量的生成采样按估计热力学稳定性而非观测布居采样。

Result: 在径向双势阱粒子、丙氨酸二肽和九肽AIB9上，SPIBER将采样亚稳态间的自由能差恢复到参考值的一个热能单位以内，并可在最多四个潜维中工作。

Conclusion: 该方法无需收敛的态布居或额外分子动力学模拟，可联合学习集体变量并估计自由能，为有限采样下分子亚稳态热力学分析提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Molecular systems have many degrees of freedom, but their metastable behavior can often be described by a few collective variables. Identifying these variables and estimating free energies along them from limited simulation data remains a challenging, important problem. Separate short trajectories may sample different metastable states without capturing transitions or establishing their relative equilibrium populations. For unbiased trajectories generated with the same Hamiltonian at a single temperature, alternate methods based on histogram reweighting cannot correct this imbalance. Here we present SPIBER, which combines the State Predictive Information Bottleneck (SPIB) with Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). SPIB uses deep learning to approximate slow degrees of freedom through a past-future information bottleneck, retaining information needed to predict future metastable states. We show that this compression limits conditional entropy variations in populated regions, allowing conditional mean potential energies, which are much easier to calculate, to be used to approximate free energy differences. Given sufficient local sampling to estimate these energies, they define the target distribution for GFlowNets, energy-based generative samplers that sample according to estimated thermodynamic stability rather than observed populations. For a particle in a radial double-well potential, for alanine dipeptide, and for the nine-residue peptide AIB9, SPIBER recovers free energy differences between sampled metastable states to within one thermal energy unit of reference values. The method combines collective-variable learning and free energy estimation in up to four latent dimensions, without requiring converged state populations or additional molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>


### [115] [Can Chemically Inspired Parameter Initialization Mitigate Barren Plateaus in Variational Quantum Eigensolvers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22729)
*Zhangyu Yang,Jinzhao Sun,Jianpeng Chen,Weitang Li,Zhigang Shuai*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究考察分子UCCSD-VQE中化学启发式初始化对局部贫瘠高原的缓解作用，发现其局部梯度方差随体系规模呈多项式衰减，而非随机初始化下的指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE在近-term NISQ设备上具有分子电子结构计算潜力，但贫瘠高原导致梯度消失并严重阻碍规模化优化。现有研究多关注随机初始化电路和全局梯度行为，而实际量子化学VQE通常采用化学启发式初始化并在HF或MP2派生态附近进行局部优化，这种初始化如何缓解局部贫瘠高原仍不明确。

Method: 在分子UCCSD-VQE中研究局部贫瘠高原的发生，同时考虑化学启发式初始化点以及沿相应优化轨迹的迭代点，并分析局部梯度方差随系统规模的标度行为，与随机初始化对照进行比较。

Result: 在所用基准测试中，化学初始化起点和变分轨迹的局部梯度方差随系统规模呈多项式衰减，而随机对照则表现为指数型贫瘠高原式衰减。

Conclusion: 化学初始化可缓解局部优化区域中的贫瘠高原，表明化学启发式VQE策略具有可训练性优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) have strengthened its promise for molecular electronic-structure calculations on near-term noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices. However, barren plateaus (BPs) remain a major obstacle to scaling VQE, as vanishing gradients can severely obstruct optimization at large scale. While most BP studies focus on randomly initialized circuits and global gradient behavior, practical quantum chemistry VQE typically uses chemically motivated initializations and local optimization around HF- or MP2-derived states. Although chemically informed initializations are widely expected to improve VQE trainability, how they mitigate local barren plateaus remains unclear. We therefore study the occurrence of local barren plateaus in molecular UCCSD-VQE, considering both chemically motivated initialization points and iterates along the corresponding optimization trajectories. In the benchmarks considered here, chemically initialized starting points and variational trajectories show polynomial decay of local gradient variance with system size instead of the exponential BP-like decay observed for random controls, indicating that chemical initialization mitigates barren plateaus in the local optimization regions and highlights the trainability advantage of chemically motivated VQE strategies.

</details>


### [116] [ECENet: An Edge Cluster Expansion Line-Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23134)
*R. Allen LaCour,Teresa Head-Gordon*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出边缘团簇展开（ECE）及线图神经网络 ECENet，以 O(2) 等变边中心表示构建高效、高精度的机器学习原子间势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）是经典力场和第一性原理方法的有力替代，但许多基于 O(3) 等变图神经网络的模型虽然准确，计算成本却很高，需要更高效且物理表达能力强的等变架构。

Method: 作者提出边缘团簇展开（ECE），将原子团簇展开中的环境围绕原子对之间的边而非单个原子展开，并据此构建线图神经网络 ECENet。ECENet 使用持续存在于原子间边上的 O(2) 等变特征，从而自然利用比 O(3) 操作更便宜、限制更少的 O(2) 操作；同时通过潜空间 Ewald 求和引入长程静电作用。

Result: ECENet 在 MD22 基准上达到当前最优性能，在 SPICE-MACE-OFF 数据集上训练时位于精度-成本帕累托前沿；结合长程静电后，还能准确预测分子偶极矩和液态水的红外光谱。

Conclusion: 前沿的 ECENet 架构表明，等变边中心表示可作为高效且物理表达丰富的等变 MLIPs 基础。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a promising alternative to classical force fields and first-principles theory for predicting the properties of chemical and material systems. Many MLIPs are graph neural networks with O(3)-equivariant features, whose accuracy comes at substantial computational cost. Here we introduce the edge cluster expansion (ECE), an analogue of the atomic cluster expansion in which the environment is expanded around edges between atom pairs rather than single atoms, and build upon it to develop the line-graph neural network ECENet. ECENet uses O(2)-equivariant features that persist on the edges between atoms, giving it natural access to O(2) operations that are cheaper and less restrictive than their O(3) counterparts. ECENet performs at the state of the art on the MD22 benchmark and lies on the accuracy-cost Pareto frontier when trained on the SPICE-MACE-OFF dataset. Furthermore, with long-range electrostatics implemented via latent Ewald summation, ECENet accurately predicts molecular dipole moments and the infrared spectrum of liquid water. The frontier ECENet architecture establishes equivariant edge-centered representations as an efficient and physically expressive foundation for equivariant MLIPs.

</details>


### [117] [Solvent Inertia Changes Product Probability by Relocating the Phase-Space Reactivity Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23370)
*Stephen Wiggins*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 通过改变溶剂惯性来改变分子哈密顿量的动能项，从而改变相空间流，即使势能面不变；在二自由度溶质-溶剂模型中，产物概率由确定性阈值和噪声诱导的连续转变共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常溶剂效应被归因于势能面变化，但溶剂惯性本身会改变动能项和完整哈密顿流；作者希望澄清溶剂惯性如何在不改变势能面的情况下影响反应产物概率。

Method: 采用二自由度溶质-溶剂模型，对不同溶剂质量在相同总能量下选取初始条件；识别产物-反应物转化的确定性阈值质量及其对应的过渡态周期轨道稳定流形；对溶剂动量施加弱高斯噪声，并用确定性阈值与线性响应理论预测产物概率变化的位置和宽度，最后用蒙特卡洛模拟验证。

Result: 确定性结果在阈值质量处由生成产物变为返回反应物；弱高斯噪声将该突变平滑为连续概率变化，变化质量区间宽度与噪声强度的平方根成正比。三个噪声强度下的蒙特卡洛结果与线性响应预测在采样不确定度内一致。

Conclusion: 溶剂惯性通过完整哈密顿流的相空间几何，而非势能面变化，连接至产物概率；该计算为理解溶剂动力学对反应结果的影响提供了无需拟合的线性响应框架。

Abstract: Changing solvent inertia changes the kinetic part of a molecular Hamiltonian and hence its flow in phase space, without requiring a change in the potential energy surface (PES). We study how this affects product probability in a solute-solvent model with two degrees of freedom. Initial conditions are chosen at the same total energy for each solvent mass. For this family, the deterministic outcome changes from reaching products to returning to reactants at a threshold mass. The initial condition at that mass belongs to the stable manifold of the transition-state periodic orbit. Weak Gaussian forcing of the solvent momentum replaces the abrupt change in outcome by a continuous change in product probability over a mass interval proportional to the square root of the noise strength. The deterministic threshold and a linear response calculation predict the position and width of this probability change without fitting the stochastic simulations. Monte Carlo results at three noise strengths agree with the prediction within sampling uncertainty. The calculation connects solvent inertia to product probability through the geometry of the full Hamiltonian flow, rather than through a change in the PES.

</details>


### [118] [Quantitative Classical Relaxation Dynamics of Penning-Ionized He/Ne--H$_2^+$/HD$^+$ Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23412)
*Meenu Upadhyay,Baruch Margulis,Octavio Roncero,Karl Horn,Edvardas Narevicius,Markus Meuwly*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 利用Penning电离原位生成稀有气体–H2+/HD+离子复合物，结合TKER谱与准经典轨迹模拟，揭示其振动弛豫/解离动力学及初始碰撞几何依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稀有气体–H2+/HD+复合物的长程分子间相互作用及振动弛豫和解离动力学；TKER谱对长程相互作用尤其敏感，需要从实验谱中提取微观动力学信息。

Method: 通过Penning电离原位产生离子复合物并测量H2+/HD+的TKER谱；对Ne–H2+和Ne–HD+进行准经典轨迹（QCT）模拟，与实验及含时量子模拟对比，并进行轨迹分辨分析。

Result: QCT得到的TKER分布定量重现多数实验特征的位置和相对强度，且与含时量子模拟相当；可分离(v=2)→(v'=0/1)等最终振动态信号；复合物寿命与最终转动量子数j'相关，低j'主要来自初始轴向接近，高j'主要来自初始T形接近。

Conclusion: QCT模拟能有效解释TKER谱、振动弛豫通道及空间/时间依赖机制，表明初始碰撞几何决定最终转动态分布和复合物寿命。

Abstract: Penning ionization allows {\it in situ} generation of ionic complexes to launch vibrational relaxation and dissociation dynamics for rare gas--H$_2^+$/HD$^+$ complexes. The ensuing translational kinetic energy release (TKER) spectra characterizing the diatomic cation are a particularly sensitive way to probe the long range part of the intermolecular interactions. TKER distributions from quasi-classical trajectory (QCT) simulations for the Ne--H$_2^+$ and Ne--HD$^+$ complexes quantitatively match measured positions and relative intensities of most experimentally observed features and are also on par with time-dependent quantum simulations. Specifically, the QCT-simulations allow to separate signatures arising from H$_2^+$/HD$^+$ relaxation into different final vibrational states, such as for $(v=2) \rightarrow (v' = 0/1 )$. Trajectory-resolved QCT analysis also provides mechanistic interpretations of the spatial and time-dependent dynamics underlying the TKER distributions. Rare gas--H$_2^+$/HD$^+$ complex lifetimes directly correlate with the final rotational quantum number $j'$ of the diatomic. Low-$j'$ products are associated primarily with an initial axial approach of the rare gas atom, whereas formation of higher-$j'$ states predominantly results from an initial T-shaped approach geometry.

</details>


### [119] [Attosecond charge migration timescales are dominated by transition dipoles, not correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23615)
*Km Akanksha Dubey,Ofer Neufeld*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文用从头算含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）研究BrC4H中的阿秒电荷迁移（CM），发现空穴矩的主频近似普适，而空穴密度演化时标依赖化学条件与电子关联；半解析理论表明其由分子偶极/跃迁偶极的频率选择决定，类似光学选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷迁移在太阳能转换、光合作用和辐射损伤中至关重要，但其基本物理化学机制仍未完全理解，尤其需要深入认识电子关联的作用以及哪些化学属性决定CM时标。

Method: 采用从头算含时密度泛函理论研究基准分子BrC4H的CM；系统探索不同电子与结构初始条件，并比较不同电子关联程度的方法；进一步用半解析理论在阳离子参考系中重构空穴矩。

Result: 发现空穴矩（联系实验可观测量）的主频近似独立于初始条件、结构特征和关联程度；相反，空穴密度演化的时标随化学条件和关联水平变化。半解析重构表明，CM的阿秒时标由分子偶极滤波特定频率响应决定，可类比光学选择定则。

Conclusion: 结果提供对CM物理的关键认识，有助于解释阿秒实验，并可通过裁剪跃迁偶极来工程化调控电荷迁移时标。

Abstract: Attosecond charge migration (CM) is an ultrafast process occurring when a molecule is irradiated by ultrashort laser pulses, creating a localized hole. The hole propagates rapidly through the molecule, generating electric currents and transferring charge across the molecular backbone. CM is a key ingredient in solar energy conversion, photosynthesis, and radiation damage. Despite its importance and intensive research, the fundamental physical and chemical mechanisms of CM remain not fully understood. Especially, a deeper insight into the role correlations play in the dynamics, and which chemical attributes determine CM timescales, is needed. Here we study with \textit{ab-initio} time-dependent density functional theory CM in the benchmark molecule, BrC$_4$H. We thoroughly explore CM under different initial conditions at the electronic and structural levels, including with theories of varying degrees of electronic correlations. We uncover a universal behavior where the hole moment (connecting to experimental observables) dominant frequency is roughly independent of all of these characteristics. In contrast, the timescales of the hole density evolution do vary with the chemical conditions and level of correlations. Employing a semi-analytical theory that reconstructs the hole moments in the cationic reference frame, we show that the attosecond timescale of CM is determined by molecular dipoles that filter out specific frequency responses with an analogy to optical selection rules. Our results provide essential insight into CM physics, which should be useful for interpreting attosecond experiments and engineering CM timescales by tailoring transition dipoles.

</details>


### [120] [AI-assisted analytical theory in soft matter and multiphysics: new results and lessons from diffusiophoresis and bipolar membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23930)
*Ankur Gupta*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个AI辅助的分析理论框架，通过诚实规则、聚焦关键文献、先验检查、单任务会话与运行日志以及人类审批，在软物质与多物理场中快速产生新解析知识，同时强调防止认知卸载并保持透明与可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在加速软物质与多物理场领域的分析理论发展，借助AI提升复杂数学推导效率，同时确保科研过程的严谨性、可复现性和人类主导决策。

Method: 框架强调五点：设定诚实规则；聚焦少量关键论文以凝练问题；事先指定检查项；每次会话只处理一个任务并记录任务、检查、修正与会话日志以避免错误传播；所有决策由人类批准，AI主要负责执行。该框架应用于两个问题：任意数量电解质与任意Debye长度下球形颗粒的扩散泳；不含水电离动力学的双极膜I-V曲线解析描述，包括过极限电流。

Result: 两个通常各需数月至一年工作量的高级分析问题，在数天内得到解决；同时透明呈现了AI的使用方式，并初步讨论了研究生教育中整合AI工具的问题。

Conclusion: 效率提升应谨慎对待，避免认知卸载，确保AI辅助结果用于提炼人类可读的洞见，而非仅报告新的数学公式；AI使用需透明，并应探索其在理论科研与研究生教育中的合理整合。

Abstract: We present a framework to advance analytical theory in the area of soft matter and multiphysics using assistance from AI. The framework emphasizes (i) setting "honesty rules", (ii) focusing on a few key papers that are used to formulate a problem of interest, (iii) specifying checks a priori, (iv) using only one task per session and keeping a running log of tasks, checks, corrections, and sessions so that a mistake is not propagated through a project, and (v) ensuring all decisions are approved by humans while AI focuses primarily on execution. The framework is applied to two distinct problems to create new knowledge, including (i) diffusiophoresis of a spherical particle in the presence of an arbitrary number of electrolytes at arbitrary Debye lengths, and (ii) analytical description of I-V curves, including overlimiting current, in bipolar membranes without including the water dissociation kinetics. Both of these problems are advanced analyses and would each typically require several months to a year's worth of effort, but were solved in a matter of a few days. However, the increased efficiency should be cautiously navigated to avoid cognitive offloading and ensure that AI-assisted results are used to extract human-readable insights instead of just reporting a new mathematical formula. The usage of AI is transparently presented. Finally, we also share brief thoughts on how to integrate AI tools in theoretical research during graduate education.

</details>


### [121] [From Heuristics to Machine Learning: The Performance Ceiling for Single-Ion Magnets and Its Electronic Origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24038)
*Federico Zahariev,Regina Pereyra,Vassiliki-Alexandra Glezakou,Durga Paudyal*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文检验仅凭合成前可得的几何结构信息能否用机器学习预测镧系单离子磁体（SIM），发现几何描述仅略优于“Dy3+即SIM”规则，失败源于隧道效应与多核/自由基集体弛豫；需引入电子结构与连接性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习有望加速SIM发现，但关键问题是：合成前可获得的分子结构信息是否足以预测SIM，以及几何模型失败的原因和提升筛选可靠性的途径。

Method: 基于SIMDAVIS 1.2.1中1215个镧系配合物，比较三种逐级增强的结构描述：配位点表格特征、配位多面体连续对称性度量、完整三维原子排布；用多参考从头算计算并结合高置信错误样本的结构分析解释错误，并评估高置信筛选与新描述符策略。

Result: 三种几何描述准确率均约76%，仅略高于单一规则“Dy3+预测为SIM”的71%。几何模型漏掉的SIM多为场诱导弛豫体，其基态Kramers双重态易发生量子隧穿，几何描述符不可见；假阳性多含多个镧系中心或自由基，弛豫为集体行为，超出单离子图像。限制于高预测置信度样本时准确率升至88%，同时保留48%数据集。

Conclusion: 仅靠几何方法无法获取识别SIM所需的电子结构与连接性信息；几何模型仍可作为高置信预筛。提出结合核数与自由基简单过滤及从头算配体场描述符的策略，以更可靠地预测/发现SIM。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is expected to speed up the discovery of single-ion magnets (SIMs), but does the structural information available before synthesis allow such predictions? For 1215 lanthanide complexes from the SIMDAVIS 1.2.1 database we compared three increasing levels of structural description: tabular features of the coordination site, continuous symmetry measures of the coordination polyhedron, and the complete 3D arrangement of atoms. All three converge to an accuracy near 76%, only slightly above the 71% of the single rule "predict SIM for Dy3+". To explain the failures, we combined multireference ab initio calculations with an inspection of the structures behind the high-confidence errors. The SIMs missed by the geometric models are field-induced relaxers whose ground Kramers doublets are prone to tunnelling, a property invisible to geometric descriptors. Many false positives contain several lanthanide centers or radicals, so their relaxation is collective and outside the single-ion picture. The electronic-structure and connectivity information needed to identify SIMs is therefore not accessible to geometric methods alone. Geometric models remain useful: restricting the screening to compounds with high prediction confidence raises the accuracy to 88% while retaining 48% of the dataset. Building on the analysis of the failures, we propose a strategy that combines simple filters for nuclearity and for radicals with ligand-field descriptors from ab initio calculations.

</details>


### [122] [Composition-Dependent Self-Diffusion Coefficients in Liquid Mixtures from Hybrid Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24599)
*Jens Wagner,Thomas Specht,Hans Hasse,Fabian Jirasek*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出HADES混合模型，将Stokes-Einstein方程与深度学习结合，可预测多组分液体混合物在任意组成和温度下的浓度依赖自扩散系数，仅需SMILES分子结构和纯组分粘度，性能显著优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自扩散系数是分子运动性的关键描述符，但实验数据稀缺，亟需可靠预测方法；此前ESE模型仅能预测纯溶剂中无限稀释溶质的自扩散系数，需进一步扩展到浓度依赖和多组分溶剂体系。

Method: HADES采用混合架构，利用deep-set神经网络在单一框架内连接纯组分预测与混合物预测；输入为组分的SMILES编码分子结构和纯组分粘度，可处理任意组分数、任意组成和温度。

Result: 在包含600个体系、2526个数据点的综合数据集上训练与评估，HADES显著优于基准预测方法；模型训练权重和源代码完全公开，并提供交互式网站 https://ml-prop.mv.rptu.de/。

Conclusion: HADES为多组分液体混合物中自扩散系数预测提供了广泛适用且物理一致的解决方案，并具备开放可及性。

Abstract: Self-diffusion coefficients are key descriptors of molecular mobility, yet experimental data remain scarce, highlighting the need for reliable prediction methods. In previous work, we introduced the hybrid Enhanced Stokes-Einstein (ESE) model, which advanced the state of the art in the physically consistent prediction of self-diffusion coefficients of solutes at infinite dilution in pure solvents by integrating the Stokes-Einstein equation with machine learning (ML). Here, we extend this approach to concentration-dependent self-diffusion coefficients and multicomponent solvents with HADES. This hybrid architecture leverages a deep-set neural network to connect pure-component and mixture prediction within a single framework. HADES predicts self-diffusion coefficients in liquid mixtures with any number of components at any composition and temperature. The only required inputs are SMILES-encoded molecular structures of the components and the pure-component viscosities, making the method broadly applicable. Trained and evaluated on a comprehensive dataset of 2526 data points for 600 systems, HADES significantly outperforms benchmark prediction methods. The trained model and its source code are fully disclosed, and the application is available via an interactive website https://ml-prop.mv.rptu.de/.

</details>
